});

test('추천(자투리 150x65 면): 적합 도안을 필요 면적이 큰 순서로', async () => {
  const data = await recs(await mk({ title: '추천 자투리' }));
  assert.equal(data.available, true);
  assert.deepEqual(names(data), ['쿠션 커버', '에코백', '파우치', '티코스터']);  // 앞치마는 세로 82cm 가 필요해서 제외
  assert.ok(data.items.every((i) => i.judgement === 'SUITABLE'));
  assert.equal(data.items[1].required_length_cm, null);  // 자투리는 마 수·금액 없음
  assert.equal(data.items[1].estimated_price, null);
  assert.equal(data.message, null);
  assert.ok(data.notices[0].startsWith('실물 확인 권장'));

  const ecobag = data.items.find((i) => i.pattern_name === '에코백');
  assert.deepEqual(ecobag.reasons.map((r) => r.criterion), ['WIDTH', 'LENGTH', 'MATERIAL', 'THICKNESS']);
  assert.equal(ecobag.reasons[1].required, 48);
  assert.equal(ecobag.reasons[1].actual, 65);
  assert.deepEqual(ecobag.reasons[2].required, ['COTTON', 'LINEN', 'BLEND']);
  assert.equal(ecobag.reasons[2].actual, 'COTTON');
});

test('추천(롤 린넨 6,000원): 도안마다 필요 길이·구매 길이·마 수·예상 금액, 마 수가 적은 순서', async () => {
  const data = await recs(await mk({ title: '추천 롤', sale_type: 'ROLL', price: 6000, remaining_length_cm: 900, detail: { material: 'LINEN' } }));
  assert.deepEqual(names(data), ['파우치', '티코스터', '쿠션 커버', '에코백', '앞치마']);
  const ecobag = data.items.find((i) => i.pattern_name === '에코백');
  assert.equal(ecobag.required_length_cm, 210);
  assert.equal(ecobag.purchase_length_cm, 270);
  assert.equal(ecobag.yards, 3);
  assert.equal(ecobag.estimated_price, 18000);
});

test('추천: 두께·소재가 안 맞는 도안은 빠진다', async () => {
  assert.deepEqual(names(await recs(await mk({ title: '두꺼운 면', detail: { thickness: 'THICK' } }))), ['쿠션 커버', '에코백', '티코스터']);  // 파우치는 얇음·보통만
  assert.deepEqual(names(await recs(await mk({ title: '폴리', detail: { material: 'POLYESTER' } }))), ['쿠션 커버', '파우치']);   // 에코백·티코스터는 폴리 불가
});

test('추천: 소재를 모르면 모두 확인 필요(최대 3개)이고 안내가 붙는다', async () => {
  const data = await recs(await mk({ title: '소재 미확인', detail: { material: 'UNKNOWN' } }));
  assert.equal(data.items.length, 3);
  assert.ok(data.items.every((i) => i.judgement === 'NEEDS_CHECK'));
  assert.ok(data.notices.includes('소재를 판매자에게 확인해 보세요'));
  assert.equal(data.items[0].reasons.find((r) => r.criterion === 'MATERIAL').met, null);
});

test('추천: 만들 수 있는 도안이 없으면 안내 문구', async () => {
  const data = await recs(await mk({ title: '아주 작은 나일론', detail: { material: 'NYLON', thickness: 'THIN', width_cm: 30, length_cm: 30 } }));
  assert.equal(data.available, true);
  assert.deepEqual(data.items, []);
  assert.equal(data.message, '이 원단으로 만들 수 있는 도안이 없어요');
});

test('추천: 작은 자투리는 작은 도안만 (30x30 면 -> 티코스터)', async () => {
  const data = await recs(await mk({ title: '작은 면', detail: { width_cm: 30, length_cm: 30 } }));
  assert.deepEqual(names(data), ['티코스터']);
});

test('추천: 구매할 수 없는 원단은 available false (게시 전, 예약 중, 롤 90cm 미만, 판매 완료)', async () => {
  const unavailable = (data) => {
    assert.equal(data.available, false);
    assert.deepEqual(data.items, []);
    assert.equal(data.message, '지금은 구매할 수 없는 원단이에요');
  };

  const pending = (await api('POST', '/fabrics', { token: seller.token, body: fabricBody({ title: '게시 전' }) })).body.data.fabricId;
  unavailable(await recs(pending));

  // 자투리가 예약 중
  const scrap = await mk({ title: '예약될 자투리' });
  const buyer = await signup(api, 'BUYER', 'recbuyer');
  const r = await api('POST', '/reservations', { token: buyer.token, body: { fabric_id: scrap, pickup_at: hoursFromNow(48) } });
  assert.equal((await recs(scrap)).available, true);  // 요청만으로는 아직 구매 가능
  await api('POST', `/reservations/${r.body.data.reservationId}/accept`, { token: seller.token, body: {} });
  unavailable(await recs(scrap));

  // 롤: 구매 가능 길이가 줄어드는 만큼 추천이 줄고, 90cm 미만이면 불가
  const roll = await mk({ title: '줄어드는 롤', sale_type: 'ROLL', price: 6000, remaining_length_cm: 360, detail: { material: 'LINEN' } });
  assert.ok(names(await recs(roll)).includes('앞치마'));  // 360cm 를 모두 쓰는 앞치마
  const b2 = await signup(api, 'BUYER', 'recbuyer2');
  const big = await api('POST', '/reservations', { token: b2.token, body: { fabric_id: roll, pickup_at: hoursFromNow(48), requested_length_cm: 180 } });
  await api('POST', `/reservations/${big.body.data.reservationId}/accept`, { token: seller.token, body: {} });
  const after = await recs(roll);
  assert.deepEqual(names(after).sort(), ['쿠션 커버', '티코스터', '파우치'].sort());  // 구매 가능 180cm 이내만
  const b3 = await signup(api, 'BUYER', 'recbuyer3');
  const rest = await api('POST', '/reservations', { token: b3.token, body: { fabric_id: roll, pickup_at: hoursFromNow(48), requested_length_cm: 90 } });
  await api('POST', `/reservations/${rest.body.data.reservationId}/accept`, { token: seller.token, body: {} });
  assert.equal((await recs(roll)).available, true);  // 90cm 남음
  const b4 = await signup(api, 'BUYER', 'recbuyer4');
  const lastOne = await api('POST', '/reservations', { token: b4.token, body: { fabric_id: roll, pickup_at: hoursFromNow(48), requested_length_cm: 90 } });
  await api('POST', `/reservations/${lastOne.body.data.reservationId}/accept`, { token: seller.token, body: {} });
  unavailable(await recs(roll));  // 구매 가능 길이 0

  assert.equal((await api('GET', '/fabrics/999999/recommendations')).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await api('GET', '/fabrics/abc/recommendations')).status, 400);
});
tests/reservations.test.js (266줄) 
import { test, before, after } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  startTestServer, stopTestServer, makeApi, signup, sellerWithStore, adminToken, postedFabric, hoursFromNow,
} from './helpers.js';

let ctx;
let api;
let seller;
let otherSeller;
let admin;
before(async () => {
  ctx = await startTestServer();
  api = makeApi(ctx.baseUrl);
  seller = await sellerWithStore(api, 'fabricshop');
  otherSeller = await sellerWithStore(api, 'rival');
  admin = await adminToken(api);
});
after(async () => stopTestServer(ctx.server));

const reserve = (buyer, body) => api('POST', '/reservations', { token: buyer.token, body: { pickup_at: hoursFromNow(48), ...body } });
const act = (who, id, action, body) => api('POST', `/reservations/${id}/${action}`, { token: who.token, body });
const fabricOf = (id, token) => api('GET', `/fabrics/${id}`, { token });
const newBuyer = (name) => signup(api, 'BUYER', name);

test('자투리: 요청 -> 수락 -> 다른 요청 거절 -> 취소 -> 수령 -> 판매 완료', async () => {
  const fabricId = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '자투리 예약' });
  const [b1, b2, b3, b4] = await Promise.all(['b1', 'b2', 'b3', 'b4'].map(newBuyer));

  // 요청: 재고를 확보하지 않는다
  const r1 = await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, memo: '오후에 갈게요' });
  assert.equal(r1.status, 201);
  assert.deepEqual(r1.body.data.status, 'REQUESTED');
  assert.equal(r1.body.data.estimated_price, null);
  const id1 = r1.body.data.reservationId;

  assert.equal((await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.error.code, 'DUPLICATE_RESERVATION');
  assert.equal((await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 90 })).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  assert.equal((await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: fabricId, pickup_at: hoursFromNow(-2) })).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  assert.equal((await reserve(seller, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');  // 구매자만
  assert.equal((await api('POST', '/reservations', { body: { fabric_id: fabricId, pickup_at: hoursFromNow(48) } })).status, 401);
  assert.equal((await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: 999999 })).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: fabricId, pattern_id: 999999 })).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');

  const r2 = await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: fabricId });  // 아직 수락 전이라 다른 구매자도 요청할 수 있다
  assert.equal(r2.status, 201);
  const id2 = r2.body.data.reservationId;
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.availability.is_reserved, false);

  // 수락
  assert.equal((await act(otherSeller, id1, 'accept', {})).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');  // 다른 판매자
  assert.equal((await act(b1, id1, 'accept', {})).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');           // 구매자는 수락 못 함
  const accepted = await act(seller, id1, 'accept', {});
  assert.deepEqual(accepted.body.data, { reservationId: id1, status: 'ACCEPTED' });
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.availability.is_reserved, true);
  const inList = (await api('GET', '/fabrics?size=50')).body.data.content.find((f) => f.fabricId === fabricId);
  assert.equal(inList.is_reserved, true);  // 예약 중이어도 목록에서 빠지지 않는다

  // 수락할 때 재고를 다시 확인한다
  assert.equal((await act(seller, id2, 'accept', {})).body.error.code, 'SCRAP_ALREADY_RESERVED');
  assert.equal((await reserve(b3, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.error.code, 'SCRAP_ALREADY_RESERVED');
  assert.equal((await act(seller, id1, 'accept', {})).body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');  // 이미 수락됨

  // 거절: 사유 필수
  assert.equal((await act(seller, id2, 'reject', {})).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  assert.equal((await act(seller, id1, 'reject', { reason: '사유' })).body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');
  const rejected = await act(seller, id2, 'reject', { reason: '다른 분과 약속이 잡혔어요' });
  assert.equal(rejected.body.data.status, 'REJECTED');
  const b2List = await api('GET', '/reservations/me', { token: b2.token });
  assert.equal(b2List.body.data.content[0].reject_reason, '다른 분과 약속이 잡혔어요');

  // 수락된 예약에는 매장 주소와 픽업 안내가 함께 온다
  const b1List = await api('GET', '/reservations/me', { token: b1.token });
  assert.equal(b1List.body.data.content[0].store_address, '경기도 양주시 어딘가 1');
  assert.equal(b1List.body.data.content[0].pickup_guide, '평일 10~18시, 1층 카운터');
  assert.equal(b2List.body.data.content[0].store_address, undefined);

  // 판매자가 취소하면 확보가 풀린다
  const canceled = await act(seller, id1, 'cancel', { reason: '재고 확인이 필요해요' });
  assert.equal(canceled.body.data.status, 'CANCELED');
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.availability.is_reserved, false);
  const detail = await api('GET', `/reservations/${id1}`, { token: b1.token });
  assert.equal(detail.body.data.canceled_by, 'SELLER');
  assert.ok(detail.body.data.canceled_at);

  // 다시 예약 -> 수락 -> 다른 요청이 있는 상태에서 수령
  const r1b = await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId });
  const id1b = r1b.body.data.reservationId;
  const r4 = await reserve(b4, { fabric_id: fabricId });
  await act(seller, id1b, 'accept', {});
  assert.equal((await act(otherSeller, id1b, 'pickup')).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');
  assert.equal((await act(seller, r4.body.data.reservationId, 'pickup')).body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');  // 수락 전에는 수령 불가
  const picked = await act(seller, id1b, 'pickup');
  assert.deepEqual(picked.body.data, { reservationId: id1b, status: 'PICKED_UP' });

  // 판매 완료: 원단 SOLD_OUT, 남은 요청은 "판매 완료"로 자동 거절
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId, seller.token)).body.data.status, 'SOLD_OUT');
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).status, 404);  // 구매자에게는 보이지 않는다
  assert.ok(!(await api('GET', '/fabrics?size=50')).body.data.content.some((f) => f.fabricId === fabricId));
  const auto = await api('GET', `/reservations/${r4.body.data.reservationId}`, { token: b4.token });
  assert.equal(auto.body.data.status, 'REJECTED');
  assert.equal(auto.body.data.reject_reason, '판매 완료');

  assert.equal((await act(b1, id1b, 'cancel')).body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');  // 수령 후에는 취소 불가
  assert.equal((await reserve(b3, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.error.code, 'FABRIC_NOT_AVAILABLE');
  const edit = await api('PATCH', `/fabrics/${fabricId}`, { token: seller.token, body: { title: 'x' } });
  assert.equal(edit.body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');  // 판매 완료는 수정 불가
});

test('롤: 마 단위 요청, 확보 길이, 취소로 해제, 수령으로 차감, 남은 길이 90 미만이면 판매 완료', async () => {
  const fabricId = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, {
    title: '롤 예약', sale_type: 'ROLL', price: 6000, remaining_length_cm: 900, detail: { material: 'LINEN' },
  });
  const [b1, b2, b3] = await Promise.all(['r1', 'r2', 'r3'].map(newBuyer));
  const avail = async () => (await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.availability;

  // 요청 검사
  assert.equal((await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');  // 길이 필수
  assert.equal((await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 100 })).body.error.code, 'LENGTH_NOT_MULTIPLE_OF_90');
  assert.equal((await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 45 })).body.error.code, 'LENGTH_NOT_MULTIPLE_OF_90');
  assert.equal((await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 0 })).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  assert.equal((await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 990 })).body.error.code, 'INSUFFICIENT_LENGTH');

  // 예상 금액 = 90cm당 가격 x 마 수
  const r1 = await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 270 });
  assert.equal(r1.body.data.estimated_price, 18000);
  const r2 = await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 540 });
  assert.equal(r2.body.data.estimated_price, 36000);
  assert.deepEqual(await avail(), { is_reserved: false, available_length_cm: 900, reserved_length_cm: 0 });

  // 수락할 때마다 확보 길이가 늘고, 구매 가능 길이가 줄어든다
  await act(seller, r1.body.data.reservationId, 'accept', {});
  assert.deepEqual(await avail(), { is_reserved: true, available_length_cm: 630, reserved_length_cm: 270 });
  await act(seller, r2.body.data.reservationId, 'accept', {});
  assert.deepEqual(await avail(), { is_reserved: true, available_length_cm: 90, reserved_length_cm: 810 });
  assert.equal((await reserve(b3, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 180 })).body.error.code, 'INSUFFICIENT_LENGTH');

  // 확보한 길이보다 남은 길이를 줄일 수 없다 (판매자 수정)
  const tooLow = await api('PATCH', `/fabrics/${fabricId}`, { token: seller.token, body: { remaining_length_cm: 720 } });
  assert.equal(tooLow.body.error.code, 'INSUFFICIENT_LENGTH');

  // 구매자가 수락된 예약을 취소하면 확보가 풀린다
  const canceled = await act(b2, r2.body.data.reservationId, 'cancel', { reason: '일정이 바뀌었어요' });
  assert.equal(canceled.body.data.status, 'CANCELED');
  assert.equal((await api('GET', `/reservations/${r2.body.data.reservationId}`, { token: b2.token })).body.data.canceled_by, 'BUYER');
  assert.deepEqual(await avail(), { is_reserved: true, available_length_cm: 630, reserved_length_cm: 270 });

  // 수령: 남은 길이와 확보 길이가 함께 줄어든다
  await act(seller, r1.body.data.reservationId, 'pickup');
  assert.deepEqual(await avail(), { is_reserved: false, available_length_cm: 630, reserved_length_cm: 0 });
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.status, 'POSTED');

  // 540 수령 -> 남은 90 (게시 중 유지), 90 수령 -> 0 (판매 완료)
  const big = await reserve(b3, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 540 });
  await act(seller, big.body.data.reservationId, 'accept', {});
  await act(seller, big.body.data.reservationId, 'pickup');
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.status, 'POSTED');
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId)).body.data.availability.available_length_cm, 90);

  const last = await reserve(b1, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 90 });
  const waiting = await reserve(b2, { fabric_id: fabricId, requested_length_cm: 90 });
  await act(seller, last.body.data.reservationId, 'accept', {});
  await act(seller, last.body.data.reservationId, 'pickup');
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(fabricId, seller.token)).body.data.status, 'SOLD_OUT');
  assert.equal((await api('GET', `/reservations/${waiting.body.data.reservationId}`, { token: b2.token })).body.data.reject_reason, '판매 완료');
});

test('수락할 때 픽업 일시를 조정할 수 있고, 과거 일시는 안 된다', async () => {
  const fabricId = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '일시 조정' });
  const buyer = await newBuyer('timing');
  const r = await reserve(buyer, { fabric_id: fabricId });
  const id = r.body.data.reservationId;

  assert.equal((await act(seller, id, 'accept', { pickup_at: hoursFromNow(-1) })).body.error.code, 'VALIDATION_ERROR');
  const newTime = hoursFromNow(72);
  await act(seller, id, 'accept', { pickup_at: newTime });
  const detail = await api('GET', `/reservations/${id}`, { token: buyer.token });
  assert.equal(new Date(detail.body.data.pickup_at).getTime(), Math.floor(new Date(newTime).getTime() / 1000) * 1000);
  assert.match(detail.body.data.pickup_at, /\+09:00$/);
});

test('취소 규칙: 판매자는 수락된 예약만, 당사자만 취소할 수 있다', async () => {
  const fabricId = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '취소 규칙' });
  const [buyer, stranger] = await Promise.all([newBuyer('canceler'), newBuyer('stranger')]);
  const id = (await reserve(buyer, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.data.reservationId;

  assert.equal((await act(seller, id, 'cancel')).body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');  // 요청 상태는 판매자가 거절만 가능
  assert.equal((await act(stranger, id, 'cancel')).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');
  assert.equal((await act(otherSeller, id, 'cancel')).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');
  const canceled = await act(buyer, id, 'cancel');  // 구매자는 요청 상태에서도 취소 가능
  assert.equal(canceled.body.data.status, 'CANCELED');
  assert.equal((await act(buyer, id, 'cancel')).body.error.code, 'INVALID_STATUS');
  assert.equal((await act(seller, 999999, 'accept', {})).status, 404);
});

test('조회: 내 예약, 받은 예약(구매자 전화번호 없음), 예약 상세는 당사자만', async () => {
  const fabricId = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '조회용' });
  const pattern = (await api('GET', '/patterns')).body.data.content[0];
  const buyer = await newBuyer('viewer');
  const stranger = await newBuyer('outsider');
  await api('PATCH', '/users/me', { token: buyer.token, body: { phone_number: '010-9999-0000' } });
  const id = (await reserve(buyer, { fabric_id: fabricId, pattern_id: pattern.patternId, memo: '도안 보고 왔어요' })).body.data.reservationId;

  const mine = await api('GET', '/reservations/me', { token: buyer.token });
  const item = mine.body.data.content[0];
  assert.equal(item.reservationId, id);
  assert.equal(item.title, '조회용');
  assert.equal(item.store_name, '양주원단');
  assert.ok(item.thumbnail_url);
  assert.equal((await api('GET', '/reservations/me?status=ACCEPTED', { token: buyer.token })).body.data.totalElements, 0);
  assert.equal((await api('GET', '/reservations/me', { token: seller.token })).status, 403);

  const received = await api('GET', `/sellers/me/reservations?fabric_id=${fabricId}`, { token: seller.token });
  assert.equal(received.body.data.content.length, 1);
  assert.equal(received.body.data.content[0].memo, '도안 보고 왔어요');
  assert.equal(received.body.data.content[0].buyer_nickname, 'viewer' + (buyer.email.match(/\d+/)[0]));
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(received.body).includes('010-9999-0000'));  // 구매자 전화번호는 주지 않는다
  assert.equal((await api('GET', '/sellers/me/reservations', { token: otherSeller.token })).body.data.totalElements, 0);

  const detail = await api('GET', `/reservations/${id}`, { token: buyer.token });
  assert.equal(detail.body.data.pattern_name, pattern.pattern_name);
  assert.equal(detail.body.data.fabric.title, '조회용');
  assert.equal(detail.body.data.store.store_phone, '031-000-0000');
  assert.equal((await api('GET', `/reservations/${id}`, { token: seller.token })).status, 200);
  assert.equal((await api('GET', `/reservations/${id}`, { token: stranger.token })).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');
  assert.equal((await api('GET', `/reservations/${id}`, { token: otherSeller.token })).body.error.code, 'FORBIDDEN');
  assert.equal((await api('GET', '/reservations/999999', { token: buyer.token })).status, 404);
});

test('삭제: 진행 중 예약이 있는 원단은 삭제할 수 없다', async () => {
  const fabricId = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '삭제 보호' });
  const buyer = await newBuyer('holder');
  const id = (await reserve(buyer, { fabric_id: fabricId })).body.data.reservationId;

  const blocked = await api('DELETE', `/fabrics/${fabricId}`, { token: seller.token });
  assert.equal(blocked.status, 409);
  assert.equal(blocked.body.error.code, 'FABRIC_HAS_ACTIVE_RESERVATION');
  await act(seller, id, 'reject', { reason: '재고가 없어요' });
  assert.equal((await api('DELETE', `/fabrics/${fabricId}`, { token: seller.token })).status, 200);  // 거절되면 삭제할 수 있다
});

test('동시에 수락해도 자투리는 한 건만, 롤은 길이를 넘지 않는다', async () => {
  const scrap = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '동시 수락 자투리' });
  const [a, b] = await Promise.all([newBuyer('racea'), newBuyer('raceb')]);
  const ra = (await reserve(a, { fabric_id: scrap })).body.data.reservationId;
  const rb = (await reserve(b, { fabric_id: scrap })).body.data.reservationId;

  const results = await Promise.all([act(seller, ra, 'accept', {}), act(seller, rb, 'accept', {})]);
  const statuses = results.map((r) => r.status).sort();
  assert.deepEqual(statuses, [200, 409]);
  assert.ok(results.some((r) => r.body.error?.code === 'SCRAP_ALREADY_RESERVED'));

  const roll = await postedFabric(api, ctx.baseUrl, seller, admin, { title: '동시 수락 롤', sale_type: 'ROLL', price: 5000, remaining_length_cm: 900 });
  const [c, d] = await Promise.all([newBuyer('racec'), newBuyer('raced')]);
  const rc = (await reserve(c, { fabric_id: roll, requested_length_cm: 540 })).body.data.reservationId;
  const rd = (await reserve(d, { fabric_id: roll, requested_length_cm: 540 })).body.data.reservationId;
  const rollResults = await Promise.all([act(seller, rc, 'accept', {}), act(seller, rd, 'accept', {})]);
  assert.deepEqual(rollResults.map((r) => r.status).sort(), [200, 409]);
  assert.ok(rollResults.some((r) => r.body.error?.code === 'INSUFFICIENT_LENGTH'));
  assert.equal((await fabricOf(roll)).body.data.availability.reserved_length_cm, 540);  // 900 을 넘지 않는다

  // 같은 구매자가 동시에 두 번 눌러도 예약은 하나
  const e = await newBuyer('racee');
  const twice = await Promise.all([reserve(e, { fabric_id: roll, requested_length_cm: 90 }), reserve(e, { fabric_id: roll, requested_length_cm: 90 })]);
  assert.deepEqual(twice.map((r) => r.status).sort(), [201, 409]);
});
tests/rules.test.js (156줄) 
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { packScrap, judge, recommend } from '../src/services/recommendation.rules.js';
import { applyExtraction, nextQuestion, requiredFields } from '../src/ai/flow.js';
import { fakeProvider } from '../src/ai/fakeProvider.js';
import { toKstIso } from '../src/utils/response.js';

// ---------- 추천 판정 규칙 (DB 없이) ----------
const ecobag = {
  pattern_id: 1, pattern_name: '에코백', image_url: null,
  allowed_materials: ['COTTON', 'LINEN', 'BLEND'], allowed_thickness: ['NORMAL', 'THICK'],
  parts: [{ part_name: '몸판', width_cm: 40, height_cm: 45, quantity: 2 }, { part_name: '끈', width_cm: 8, height_cm: 60, quantity: 2 }],
};
const scrap = (o = {}) => ({ sale_type: 'SCRAP', price: 3000, material: 'COTTON', thickness: 'NORMAL', width_cm: 150, length_cm: 65, available_length_cm: null, ...o });
const roll = (o = {}) => ({ sale_type: 'ROLL', price: 6000, material: 'COTTON', thickness: 'NORMAL', width_cm: 150, length_cm: null, available_length_cm: 900, ...o });

test('자투리 배치: 줄 단위로 놓고, 천 가로에 안 들어가면 못 만든다', () => {
  const packed = packScrap(ecobag.parts, 150);
  assert.equal(packed.fits, true);
  assert.equal(packed.requiredLength, 48);
  assert.equal(packed.usedWidth, 150);
  assert.equal(packScrap([{ width_cm: 200, height_cm: 200, quantity: 1 }], 150).fits, false);
  assert.equal(packScrap([{ width_cm: 200, height_cm: 100, quantity: 1 }], 150).requiredLength, 200);  // 눕혀서 100 폭으로 넣으면 들어간다
  assert.equal(packScrap([{ width_cm: 140, height_cm: 100, quantity: 1 }], 150).requiredLength, 100);  // 회전해서 넣는다
  assert.equal(packScrap([{ width_cm: 100, height_cm: 140, quantity: 1 }], 150).requiredLength, 100);
});

test('판정: 에코백 롤 210cm -> 270cm 구매, 3마, 18,000원', () => {
  const result = judge(roll(), ecobag);
  assert.equal(result.judgement, 'SUITABLE');
  assert.equal(result.required_length_cm, 210);
  assert.equal(result.purchase_length_cm, 270);
  assert.equal(result.yards, 3);
  assert.equal(result.estimated_price, 18000);
  assert.deepEqual(result.reasons.map((r) => r.criterion), ['WIDTH', 'LENGTH', 'MATERIAL', 'THICKNESS']);
  assert.ok(result.reasons.every((r) => r.met === true));
  assert.match(result.reasons[0].text, /필요 가로 40cm 이상 → 폭 150cm \(충족\)/);
});

test('판정: 하나라도 안 맞으면 추천하지 않는다', () => {
  assert.equal(judge(scrap({ width_cm: 30, length_cm: 30 }), ecobag), null);          // 너무 작다
  assert.equal(judge(scrap({ length_cm: 40 }), ecobag), null);                         // 세로가 모자라다 (48 필요)
  assert.equal(judge(scrap({ material: 'POLYESTER' }), ecobag), null);                 // 소재가 허용 밖
  assert.equal(judge(scrap({ thickness: 'THIN' }), ecobag), null);                     // 두께가 허용 밖
  assert.equal(judge(roll({ available_length_cm: 180 }), ecobag), null);               // 구매 길이 270 > 구매 가능 180
  assert.equal(judge(roll({ width_cm: 30 }), ecobag), null);                           // 폭이 좁다
  assert.equal(judge(roll({ available_length_cm: 270 }), ecobag).purchase_length_cm, 270);  // 딱 맞으면 된다
});

test('판정: 소재를 모르면(UNKNOWN) 확인 필요, 그 밖에는 적합', () => {
  const unknown = judge(scrap({ material: 'UNKNOWN' }), ecobag);
  assert.equal(unknown.judgement, 'NEEDS_CHECK');
  assert.equal(unknown.reasons.find((r) => r.criterion === 'MATERIAL').met, null);
  assert.equal(judge(scrap(), ecobag).judgement, 'SUITABLE');
  assert.equal(judge(scrap({ material: 'UNKNOWN', thickness: 'THIN' }), ecobag), null);  // 소재를 몰라도 두께가 틀리면 못 만든다
});

test('추천: 개수 제한(적합 5, 확인 필요 3)과 정렬, 안내 문구', () => {
  const many = Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, i) => ({
    ...ecobag, pattern_id: i + 1, pattern_name: `도안${i + 1}`,
    parts: [{ part_name: '몸판', width_cm: 10, height_cm: 10 + i * 5, quantity: 1 }],
  }));
  const suitable = recommend(scrap(), many);
  assert.equal(suitable.items.length, 5);
  assert.ok(suitable.items.every((i) => i.judgement === 'SUITABLE'));
  assert.deepEqual(suitable.items.map((i) => i.patternId), [9, 8, 7, 6, 5]);  // 자투리: 필요 면적이 큰 도안부터
  assert.ok(!suitable.items.some((i) => '_sortKey' in i));

  const unknown = recommend(scrap({ material: 'UNKNOWN' }), many);
  assert.equal(unknown.items.length, 3);
  assert.ok(unknown.items.every((i) => i.judgement === 'NEEDS_CHECK'));
  assert.ok(unknown.notices.includes('소재를 판매자에게 확인해 보세요'));
  assert.ok(unknown.notices[0].startsWith('실물 확인 권장'));

  const rolls = recommend(roll(), many);
  const yards = rolls.items.map((i) => i.yards);
  assert.deepEqual(yards, [...yards].sort((a, b) => a - b));  // 롤: 필요 마 수가 적은 도안부터

  const none = recommend(scrap({ width_cm: 5, length_cm: 5 }), many);
  assert.equal(none.items.length, 0);
  assert.equal(none.message, '이 원단으로 만들 수 있는 도안이 없어요');
});

// ---------- AI 질문 흐름 (AI 없이) ----------
const empty = { draft: {}, unconfirmed: [] };

test('질문 순서: 판매 형태 -> 이름 -> 크기 -> 소재 -> 두께 -> 색 -> 하자 -> 가격', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(requiredFields({}), ['sale_type', 'title', 'width_cm', 'length_cm', 'material', 'thickness', 'color_family', 'has_defect', 'price']);
  assert.deepEqual(requiredFields({ sale_type: 'ROLL' }).slice(2, 4), ['width_cm', 'remaining_length_cm']);
  assert.ok(requiredFields({ has_defect: true }).includes('defect_note'));
  assert.equal(nextQuestion({}, []).field, 'sale_type');
  assert.deepEqual(nextQuestion({}, []).options, ['SCRAP', 'ROLL']);
  assert.equal(nextQuestion({ sale_type: 'SCRAP', title: 't', width_cm: 50, length_cm: 70, material: 'UNKNOWN' }, ['material']).field, 'thickness');
});

test('답 정리: 목록 밖 값은 UNKNOWN/빈 값 + 미확인, 모름은 미확인, 틀린 숫자는 버림', () => {
  const a = applyExtraction(empty, { fields: { material: 'SILK', thickness: 'HEAVY', width_cm: 'abc', title: '  네이비  ' }, unknown: [] });
  assert.equal(a.draft.material, 'UNKNOWN');
  assert.equal(a.draft.thickness, undefined);
  assert.equal(a.draft.width_cm, undefined);
  assert.equal(a.draft.title, '네이비');
  assert.deepEqual(a.unconfirmed.sort(), ['material', 'thickness']);

  const b = applyExtraction({ draft: { thickness: 'THIN' }, unconfirmed: [] }, { fields: {}, unknown: ['thickness', 'sale_type', 'nonsense'] });
  assert.equal(b.draft.thickness, undefined);
  assert.deepEqual(b.unconfirmed, ['thickness']);  // 판매 형태는 건너뛸 수 없고, 모르는 항목 이름은 무시

  const c = applyExtraction({ draft: {}, unconfirmed: ['thickness'] }, { fields: { thickness: 'THICK' }, unknown: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(c.unconfirmed, []);  // 값을 받으면 미확인에서 빠진다

  const d = applyExtraction({ draft: { has_defect: true, defect_note: '얼룩' }, unconfirmed: [] }, { fields: { has_defect: 'false' }, unknown: [] });
  assert.equal(d.draft.has_defect, false);
  assert.equal(d.draft.defect_note, undefined);
});

test('자투리 길이: 90 초과는 롤로 등록할지, 30 미만은 다시 묻는다', () => {
  const over = applyExtraction({ draft: { sale_type: 'SCRAP', title: '코튼' }, unconfirmed: [] }, { fields: { width_cm: 50, length_cm: 120 }, unknown: [] });
  const q = nextQuestion(over.draft, over.unconfirmed);
  assert.equal(q.field, 'sale_type');
  assert.match(q.text, /롤로 등록할까요/);

  const toRoll = applyExtraction(over, { fields: { sale_type: 'ROLL' }, unknown: [] });
  assert.equal(toRoll.draft.remaining_length_cm, 120);  // 길이를 롤의 남은 길이로 옮긴다
  assert.equal(toRoll.draft.length_cm, undefined);

  const stay = applyExtraction(over, { fields: { sale_type: 'SCRAP' }, unknown: [] });
  assert.equal(stay.draft.length_cm, undefined);
  assert.equal(nextQuestion(stay.draft, stay.unconfirmed).field, 'length_cm');

  const under = applyExtraction({ draft: { sale_type: 'SCRAP', title: '코튼', width_cm: 50 }, unconfirmed: [] }, { fields: { length_cm: 20 }, unknown: [] });
  assert.match(nextQuestion(under.draft, under.unconfirmed).text, /30cm 이상/);
});

test('롤인데 세로 길이로 읽힌 값은 남은 길이로 옮긴다', () => {
  const r = applyExtraction({ draft: { sale_type: 'ROLL' }, unconfirmed: [] }, { fields: { width_cm: 150, length_cm: 270 }, unknown: [] });
  assert.equal(r.draft.remaining_length_cm, 270);
  assert.equal(r.draft.length_cm, undefined);
});

test('가짜 AI: 말하듯 쓴 답에서 값을 뽑는다', async () => {
  const ask = (askedField, answer) => fakeProvider.extract({ askedField, answer, draft: {} });
  assert.deepEqual((await ask('width_cm', '50에 70')).fields, { width_cm: 50, length_cm: 70 });
  assert.deepEqual((await ask('width_cm', '폭 150에 3마 남았어요')).fields, { width_cm: 150, remaining_length_cm: 270 });
  assert.deepEqual((await ask('remaining_length_cm', '2.5마')).fields, { remaining_length_cm: 225 });
  assert.deepEqual((await ask('price', '3500원이요')).fields, { price: 3500 });
  assert.equal((await ask('thickness', '좀 두꺼워요')).fields.thickness, 'THICK');
  assert.deepEqual((await ask('has_defect', '없어요')).fields, { has_defect: false });
  assert.equal((await ask('has_defect', '오른쪽 아래 얼룩이 있어요')).fields.has_defect, true);
  assert.deepEqual((await ask('material', '잘 모르겠어요')).unknown, ['material']);
  assert.equal((await ask('title', '네이비 면 20수')).fields.color_family, 'NAVY');
});

test('날짜는 한국 시간 문자열로 바꾼다', () => {
  assert.equal(toKstIso(new Date('2026-10-14T06:00:00.000Z')), '2026-10-14T15:00:00+09:00');
  assert.equal(toKstIso(new Date('2026-12-31T16:30:05.123Z')), '2027-01-01T01:30:05+09:00');
});
